# Router Benchmark

## Environment

- Date: 2026-05-20
- Git commit: 96f824b4e60cae8a5c7321a36bffd393666d4333
- Node: v24.15.0
- NODE_ENV: production
- pnpm: 10.19.0
- Platform: linux x64
- CPU: AMD EPYC 9V74 80-Core Processor (4)
- Memory: 16766427136 bytes
- Package versions:
  - @builder.io/qwik: 1.19.2
  - @qwik.dev/core: 2.0.0-beta.35
  - @qwik.dev/router: 2.0.0-beta.35
  - marko: 5.38.39
  - next: 16.2.6
  - react: 19.2.6
  - react-dom: 19.2.6
  - solid-js: 1.9.12
  - solid-js-2: 2.0.0-beta.13

## Rankings

### app render 1000 nodes

Renders a production app route that emits 1,000 simple text spans.

| rank | framework | case | value | diff vs 1st | unit |
| ---: | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 1 | mreact-app-router | app render 1000 nodes | 1367 | best | ops/sec |
| 2 | mreact-app-router+log enabled | app render 1000 nodes | 1353 | -1.02% | ops/sec |
| 3 | marko-run | app render 1000 nodes | 906 | -33.72% | ops/sec |
| 4 | tanstack-start | app render 1000 nodes | 763 | -44.18% | ops/sec |
| 5 | qwik-city | app render 1000 nodes | 536 | -60.79% | ops/sec |
| 6 | solid-start | app render 1000 nodes | 434 | -68.25% | ops/sec |
| 7 | qwik-router-v2 | app render 1000 nodes | 389 | -71.54% | ops/sec |
| 8 | tanstack-start-solid | app render 1000 nodes | 333 | -75.64% | ops/sec |
| 9 | next-app-router | app render 1000 nodes | 81 | -94.07% | ops/sec |
